from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Callable
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from app.application.conversation.conversation_event_repository import (
    ConversationEventRepository,
)
from app.application.learner_context.postgresql_repository import (
    CrossChannelLearnerContextRepository,
)
from app.application.learner_context.service import LearnerContextService
from app.application.memory.memory_repository import (
    ConversationMemoryRepository,
)
from app.application.privacy.privacy_identifier_service import (
    PrivacyIdentifierService,
)
from app.application.uai.world.world_resolver import WorldResolver
from app.infrastructure.persistence.postgresql_beta_access_repository import (
    PostgreSQLTelegramBindingRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.postgresql_conversation_event_repository import (
    PostgreSQLConversationEventRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.postgresql_memory_repository import (
    PostgreSQLConversationMemoryRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.worlds.yaml_world_repository import YamlWorldRepository


def build_learner_context_service(
    *,
    telegram_bindings,
    memory_repository: ConversationMemoryRepository,
    conversation_event_repository: ConversationEventRepository,
    privacy_identifier_service: PrivacyIdentifierService,
    world_resolver: WorldResolver,
) -> LearnerContextService:
    """Compose the C4.2 cross-channel learner-context service.

    All dependencies are injected. This function creates no database
    connection and no persistence implementation.
    """
    repository = CrossChannelLearnerContextRepository(
        telegram_bindings=telegram_bindings,
        memory_repository=memory_repository,
        conversation_event_repository=conversation_event_repository,
        privacy_identifier_service=privacy_identifier_service,
        world_resolver=world_resolver,
    )

    return LearnerContextService(repository=repository)


def build_production_learner_context_service(
    *,
    session_factory: Any,
    world_resolver: WorldResolver | None = None,
    telegram_binding_repository=None,
    memory_repository: ConversationMemoryRepository | None = None,
    conversation_event_repository: ConversationEventRepository | None = None,
    privacy_identifier_service: PrivacyIdentifierService | None = None,
    world_repository_factory: Callable[[], Any] | None = None,
) -> LearnerContextService:
    """Build the production C4.2 service from existing infrastructure.

    The default path creates repositories against the supplied existing
    synchronous session factory. No new database engine is created here.

    Optional dependency arguments are exposed so application composition
    tests and future composition roots can inject already-created instances.
    """
    if session_factory is None:
        raise ValueError("session_factory is required.")

    if telegram_binding_repository is None:
        telegram_binding_repository = PostgreSQLTelegramBindingRepository(
            session_factory,
        )

    if memory_repository is None:
        memory_repository = PostgreSQLConversationMemoryRepository(
            session_factory,
        )

    if conversation_event_repository is None:
        conversation_event_repository = PostgreSQLConversationEventRepository(
            session_factory,
        )

    if privacy_identifier_service is None:
        privacy_identifier_service = PrivacyIdentifierService()

    if world_resolver is None:
        if world_repository_factory is not None:
            world_repository = world_repository_factory()
        else:
            app_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
            world_repository = YamlWorldRepository(
                worlds_path=app_root / "configuration" / "worlds",
            )

        world_resolver = WorldResolver(
            world_repository=world_repository,
        )

    return build_learner_context_service(
        telegram_bindings=telegram_binding_repository,
        memory_repository=memory_repository,
        conversation_event_repository=conversation_event_repository,
        privacy_identifier_service=privacy_identifier_service,
        world_resolver=world_resolver,
    )


__all__ = [
    "build_learner_context_service",
    "build_production_learner_context_service",
]

"""Application service for catalogue-to-runtime World resolution."""
from __future__ import annotations
from app.application.uai.world.world_resolver import WorldResolver
from .repositories import LearningWorldCatalogueRepository

class LearningWorldCatalogueService:
    """Apply catalogue policy and delegate runtime resolution to WorldResolver."""
    def __init__(self, *, repository: LearningWorldCatalogueRepository, world_resolver: WorldResolver) -> None:
        if repository is None:
            raise ValueError("repository cannot be None.")
        if world_resolver is None:
            raise ValueError("world_resolver cannot be None.")
        self._repository = repository
        self._world_resolver = world_resolver

    def get_by_identifier(self, identifier: str):
        if not identifier or not identifier.strip():
            raise ValueError("catalogue identifier is required.")
        return self._repository.get_by_identifier(identifier.strip())

    def resolve_runtime_world(self, catalogue_identifier: str):
        entry = self.get_by_identifier(catalogue_identifier)
        if entry is None:
            raise ValueError(f"Learning World not found: {catalogue_identifier.strip()}")
        if not entry.enabled:
            raise ValueError(f"Learning World is disabled: {entry.identifier}")
        if not entry.runtime_world_id:
            raise ValueError(f"Learning World has no runtime configuration: {entry.identifier}")
        return self._world_resolver.resolve(entry.runtime_world_id)

"""SQLAlchemy model for the authoritative Learning World catalogue."""
from __future__ import annotations
from datetime import datetime
from uuid import UUID
from sqlalchemy import Boolean, DateTime, String
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column
from .database import Base

class LearningWorldModel(Base):
    __tablename__ = "learning_worlds"

    id: Mapped[UUID] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    identifier: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(100), nullable=False, unique=True, index=True)
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(150), nullable=False)
    description: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(500), nullable=False)
    language: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(100), nullable=False, index=True)
    version: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(50), nullable=False)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(50), nullable=False)
    enabled: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False)
    world_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(50), nullable=False)
    learning_domain: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(100), nullable=False)
    runtime_world_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(100), nullable=True, unique=True, index=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)

----- FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\application\world_catalogue\test_learning_world_catalogue_models.py -----
import pytest
from app.application.world_catalogue.models import LearningWorldCatalogueEntry

def values():
    return dict(identifier="french", name="French World", description="French language learning world.", language="fr", version="1.0.0", status="published", enabled=True, world_type="language", learning_domain="language_learning", runtime_world_id="french_world")

def test_runtime_mapping_may_be_none():
    data = values(); data["runtime_world_id"] = None
    assert LearningWorldCatalogueEntry(**data).runtime_world_id is None

@pytest.mark.parametrize("field", ["identifier", "name", "version", "status", "world_type", "learning_domain"])
def test_required_text_fields_reject_blank(field):
    data = values(); data[field] = "   "
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        LearningWorldCatalogueEntry(**data)

def test_blank_runtime_mapping_is_rejected():
    data = values(); data["runtime_world_id"] = "   "
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="runtime_world_id"):
        LearningWorldCatalogueEntry(**data)

----- FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\application\world_catalogue\test_learning_world_catalogue_service.py -----
from dataclasses import dataclass
import pytest
from app.application.world_catalogue.models import LearningWorldCatalogueEntry
from app.application.world_catalogue.service import LearningWorldCatalogueService

@dataclass
class FakeRepository:
    entry: LearningWorldCatalogueEntry | None
    def get_by_identifier(self, identifier: str):
        return self.entry

class FakeWorldResolver:
    def __init__(self, result=None):
        self.result = result
        self.requested_ids = []
    def resolve(self, world_id: str):
        self.requested_ids.append(world_id)
        return self.result

def make_entry(**changes):
    values = dict(identifier="french", name="French World", description="French language learning world.", language="fr", version="1.0.0", status="published", enabled=True, world_type="language", learning_domain="language_learning", runtime_world_id="french_world")
    values.update(changes)
    return LearningWorldCatalogueEntry(**values)

def test_resolve_uses_explicit_runtime_mapping():
    resolver = FakeWorldResolver("context")
    service = LearningWorldCatalogueService(repository=FakeRepository(make_entry()), world_resolver=resolver)
    assert service.resolve_runtime_world("french") == "context"
    assert resolver.requested_ids == ["french_world"]

def test_disabled_world_is_rejected():
    service = LearningWorldCatalogueService(repository=FakeRepository(make_entry(enabled=False)), world_resolver=FakeWorldResolver())
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="disabled"):
        service.resolve_runtime_world("french")

def test_unmapped_world_is_rejected():
    service = LearningWorldCatalogueService(repository=FakeRepository(make_entry(runtime_world_id=None)), world_resolver=FakeWorldResolver())
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="no runtime configuration"):
        service.resolve_runtime_world("french")

def test_unknown_world_is_rejected():
    service = LearningWorldCatalogueService(repository=FakeRepository(None), world_resolver=FakeWorldResolver())
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="not found"):
        service.resolve_runtime_world("french")

def test_blank_identifier_is_rejected():
    service = LearningWorldCatalogueService(repository=FakeRepository(None), world_resolver=FakeWorldResolver())
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="required"):
        service.resolve_runtime_world("   ")

----- FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\infrastructure\persistence\test_in_memory_voice_call_event_repository.py -----
"""
Tests for InMemoryVoiceCallEventRepository.

Validates:
- Event persistence.
- Event retrieval.
- Multiple events isolation.
- Event identity preservation.
"""

from app.infrastructure.persistence.in_memory_voice_call_event_repository import (
    InMemoryVoiceCallEventRepository,
)


from app.domain.uai.voice.voice_call_event import (
    VoiceCallEvent,
    VoiceCallEventType,
)



def create_event(
    *,
    event_id: str,
    voice_call_id: str = "vc-001",
):
    """
    Create test event.
    """

    return (
        VoiceCallEvent.create(
            event_id=event_id,
            voice_call_id=voice_call_id,
            learner_id="learner-001",
            character_id="yeti",
            event_type=(
                VoiceCallEventType.CREATED
            ),
        )
    )



def test_save_event():

    repository = (
        InMemoryVoiceCallEventRepository()
    )


    event = create_event(
        event_id="event-001",
    )


    saved = (
        repository.save(
            event=event,
        )
    )


    assert saved == event


    events = (
        repository.get_all()
    )


    assert len(events) == 1


    assert events[0].event_id == (
        "event-001"
    )



def test_get_events_by_voice_call_id():

    repository = (
        InMemoryVoiceCallEventRepository()
    )


    repository.save(
        event=create_event(
            event_id="event-001",
            voice_call_id="vc-001",
        )
    )


    repository.save(
        event=create_event(
            event_id="event-002",
            voice_call_id="vc-002",
        )
    )


    events = (
        repository.get_by_voice_call_id(
            voice_call_id="vc-001",
        )
    )


    assert len(events) == 1


    assert events[0].voice_call_id == (
        "vc-001"
    )


    assert events[0].event_id == (
        "event-001"
    )



def test_multiple_events_same_voice_call_are_preserved():

    repository = (
        InMemoryVoiceCallEventRepository()
    )


    repository.save(
        event=create_event(
            event_id="event-created",
            voice_call_id="vc-001",
        )
    )


    repository.save(
        event=(
            VoiceCallEvent.create(
                event_id="event-accepted",
                voice_call_id="vc-001",
                learner_id="learner-001",
                character_id="yeti",
                event_type=(
                    VoiceCallEventType.ACCEPTED
                ),
            )
        )
    )


    events = (
        repository.get_by_voice_call_id(
            voice_call_id="vc-001",
        )
    )


    assert len(events) == 2


    assert events[0].event_id == (
        "event-created"
    )


    assert events[1].event_id == (
        "event-accepted"
    )



def test_unknown_voice_call_returns_empty_list():

    repository = (
        InMemoryVoiceCallEventRepository()
    )


    events = (
        repository.get_by_voice_call_id(
            voice_call_id="unknown",
        )
    )


    assert events == []

----- FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\infrastructure\persistence\test_in_memory_voice_call_invitation_repository.py -----
"""
Tests for InMemory Voice Call Invitation Repository.

Validates the infrastructure persistence
adapter for voice call invitations.

Responsibilities tested:
- Save invitation.
- Retrieve invitation.
- Delete invitation.
- Clear repository state.
"""

from datetime import (
    datetime,
    timedelta,
    timezone,
)


from app.infrastructure.persistence.in_memory_voice_call_invitation_repository import (
    InMemoryVoiceCallInvitationRepository,
)


from app.domain.uai.voice.voice_call_invitation import (
    VoiceCallInvitation,
    VoiceCallInvitationStatus,
)



def create_repository():

    return (
        InMemoryVoiceCallInvitationRepository()
    )



def create_invitation():

    now = datetime.now(
        timezone.utc
    )


    return VoiceCallInvitation(
        id="inv-001",
        voice_call_id="vc-001",
        learner_id="learner-001",
        character_id="yeti",
        channel="notification",
        status=(
            VoiceCallInvitationStatus.CREATED
        ),
        created_at=now,
        expires_at=(
            now
            +
            timedelta(
                minutes=30
            )
        ),
    )



def test_save_and_retrieve_invitation():

    repository = create_repository()


    invitation = create_invitation()


    result = repository.save(
        invitation=invitation,
    )


    assert result == (
        invitation
    )


    stored = repository.get_by_id(
        invitation_id="inv-001",
    )


    assert stored is not None


    assert stored.id == (
        "inv-001"
    )


    assert stored.voice_call_id == (
        "vc-001"
    )



def test_unknown_invitation_returns_none():

    repository = create_repository()


    result = repository.get_by_id(
        invitation_id="unknown",
    )


    assert result is None



def test_delete_invitation():

    repository = create_repository()


    invitation = create_invitation()


    repository.save(
        invitation=invitation,
    )


    repository.delete(
        invitation_id="inv-001",
    )


    result = repository.get_by_id(
        invitation_id="inv-001",
    )


    assert result is None



def test_delete_unknown_invitation_is_safe():

    repository = create_repository()


    repository.delete(
        invitation_id="unknown",
    )


    assert repository.get_by_id(
        invitation_id="unknown",
    ) is None



def test_clear_repository():

    repository = create_repository()


    invitation = create_invitation()


    repository.save(
        invitation=invitation,
    )


    repository.clear()


    result = repository.get_by_id(
        invitation_id="inv-001",
    )


    assert result is None

from app.infrastructure.persistence import model_registry
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import Base
from app.infrastructure.persistence.learning_world_models import (
    LearningWorldModel,
)


def test_learning_world_model_uses_canonical_base_metadata() -> None:
    assert LearningWorldModel.metadata is Base.metadata


def test_learning_world_model_is_registered_in_canonical_metadata() -> None:
    assert "learning_worlds" in Base.metadata.tables
    assert Base.metadata.tables["learning_worlds"] is (
        LearningWorldModel.__table__
    )


def test_model_registry_exports_learning_world_model() -> None:
    assert model_registry.LearningWorldModel is LearningWorldModel


def test_learning_world_model_contains_runtime_world_mapping_column() -> None:
    table = Base.metadata.tables["learning_worlds"]

    assert "runtime_world_id" in table.columns
    assert table.columns["runtime_world_id"].nullable is True

----- FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\infrastructure\persistence\test_p1_6_2b_durable_usage_completeness.py -----
from __future__ import annotations

import os
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from decimal import Decimal

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text

from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import (
    create_session_factory,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.postgresql_provider_usage_repository import (
    PostgreSQLProviderUsageRepository,
)
from src.ai_runtime.providers.usage.models import ProviderUsageEvent


def _require_database_url() -> str:
    from dotenv import load_dotenv

    load_dotenv()

    value = os.getenv("DATABASE_URL")

    if not value or not value.startswith("postgresql"):
        pytest.skip("PostgreSQL DATABASE_URL is not configured.")

    return value


def test_repository_round_trips_total_tokens_and_provider_request_id() -> None:
    url = _require_database_url()

    engine = create_engine(
        url,
        pool_pre_ping=True,
    )

    try:
        session_factory = create_session_factory(engine)
        repository = PostgreSQLProviderUsageRepository(session_factory)

        table_exists = engine.connect().execute(
            text(
                "SELECT to_regclass('public.provider_usage_events')"
            )
        ).scalar_one_or_none()

        if table_exists is None:
            pytest.skip(
                "provider_usage_events table is not migrated yet."
            )

        repository.clear()

        event = ProviderUsageEvent(
            provider="p1_6_2b_test_provider",
            model="p1_6_2b_test_model",
            capability="conversation",
            functionality="chat",
            agent="conversation_agent",
            input_tokens=11,
            output_tokens=7,
            total_tokens=18,
            latency_ms=123,
            estimated_cost=Decimal("0.00123456"),
            quality_score=91.5,
            success=True,
            provider_request_id="req-p1-6-2b",
            created_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        )

        repository.save(event)

        restored = repository.get_all()

        assert len(restored) == 1

        restored_event = restored[0]

        assert restored_event.provider == event.provider
        assert restored_event.model == event.model
        assert restored_event.input_tokens == event.input_tokens
        assert restored_event.output_tokens == event.output_tokens
        assert restored_event.total_tokens == event.total_tokens
        assert (
            restored_event.provider_request_id
            == event.provider_request_id
        )
        assert restored_event.latency_ms == event.latency_ms
        assert restored_event.estimated_cost == event.estimated_cost
        assert restored_event.quality_score == event.quality_score
        assert restored_event.success is event.success

    finally:
        engine.dispose()

----- FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\infrastructure\persistence\test_postgresql_functionality_control_repository.py -----
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import Base
from app.infrastructure.persistence.functionality_control_records import (
    FunctionalityControlAuditRecord,
    FunctionalityControlStateRecord,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.postgresql_functionality_control_repository import (
    PostgreSQLFunctionalityControlRepository,
)


def create_repository():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite+pysqlite:///:memory:", future=True)
    Base.metadata.create_all(
        engine,
        tables=[
            FunctionalityControlStateRecord.__table__,
            FunctionalityControlAuditRecord.__table__,
        ],
    )

    session_factory = sessionmaker(
        bind=engine,
        expire_on_commit=False,
    )

    return PostgreSQLFunctionalityControlRepository(session_factory), session_factory


def test_missing_functionality_state_defaults_to_enabled():
    repository, _ = create_repository()

    assert repository.get_availability(
        environment="kids",
        functionality_id="conversation",
    ) == "enabled"


def test_record_transition_persists_state_and_audit():
    repository, session_factory = create_repository()
    changed_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

    result = repository.record_transition(
        functionality_id="conversation",
        environment="kids",
        availability="disabled",
        action="stop",
        reason="Provider incident",
        changed_at=changed_at,
        changed_by="operator",
    )

    assert result.functionality_id == "conversation"
    assert result.environment == "kids"
    assert result.availability == "disabled"
    assert result.action == "stop"

    assert repository.get_availability(
        environment="kids",
        functionality_id="conversation",
    ) == "disabled"

    last_action = repository.get_last_action(
        environment="kids",
        functionality_id="conversation",
    )

    assert last_action is not None
    assert last_action.action == "stop"
    assert last_action.reason == "Provider incident"
    assert last_action.changed_by == "operator"

    with session_factory() as session:
        audit_count = session.scalar(
            select(func.count())
            .select_from(FunctionalityControlAuditRecord)
        )

        state_count = session.scalar(
            select(func.count())
            .select_from(FunctionalityControlStateRecord)
        )

    assert audit_count == 1
    assert state_count == 1


def test_second_transition_updates_state_and_appends_audit():
    repository, session_factory = create_repository()
    first_time = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

    repository.record_transition(
        functionality_id="telegram",
        environment="adults",
        availability="disabled",
        action="stop",
        reason="Maintenance",
        changed_at=first_time,
        changed_by="operator",
    )

    second_time = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

    repository.record_transition(
        functionality_id="telegram",
        environment="adults",
        availability="enabled",
        action="resume",
        reason=None,
        changed_at=second_time,
        changed_by="admin",
    )

    assert repository.get_availability(
        environment="adults",
        functionality_id="telegram",
    ) == "enabled"

    last_action = repository.get_last_action(
        environment="adults",
        functionality_id="telegram",
    )

    assert last_action is not None
    assert last_action.action == "resume"
    assert last_action.changed_by == "admin"

    with session_factory() as session:
        audit_count = session.scalar(
            select(func.count())
            .select_from(FunctionalityControlAuditRecord)
        )

        state_count = session.scalar(
            select(func.count())
            .select_from(FunctionalityControlStateRecord)
        )

    assert audit_count == 2
    assert state_count == 1

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from uuid import UUID, uuid4

import pytest

from app.application.private_chat.models import PrivateChatMessage
from app.infrastructure.persistence.postgresql_private_chat_repository import (
    PostgreSQLPrivateChatRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.private_chat_models import (
    PrivateChatConversationRecord,
)


class FakeSession:
    """Minimal session double for repository unit tests."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.added: list[object] = []
        self.flushed = 0
        self.scalar_result: object | None = None
        self.scalars_result: list[object] = []

    def add(self, record: object) -> None:
        if isinstance(record, PrivateChatConversationRecord):
            if record.conversation_id is None:
                record.conversation_id = uuid4()

        self.added.append(record)

    def flush(self) -> None:
        self.flushed += 1

    def scalar(self, _statement: object) -> object | None:
        return self.scalar_result

    def scalars(self, _statement: object) -> "FakeScalars":
        return FakeScalars(self.scalars_result)


class FakeScalars:
    def __init__(self, records: list[object]) -> None:
        self._records = records

    def all(self) -> list[object]:
        return self._records


def make_conversation_record(
    *,
    conversation_id: UUID | None = None,
    user_id: str = "user-1",
    world_id: str = "polish_world",
) -> PrivateChatConversationRecord:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

    return PrivateChatConversationRecord(
        conversation_id=conversation_id or uuid4(),
        user_id=user_id,
        world_id=world_id,
        created_at=now,
        updated_at=now,
    )


def test_create_conversation_persists_owned_world_conversation() -> None:
    session = FakeSession()
    repository = PostgreSQLPrivateChatRepository(session)

    result = repository.create_conversation(
        user_id="user-1",
        world_id="polish_world",
    )

    assert result.user_id == "user-1"
    assert result.world_id == "polish_world"
    assert result.conversation_id is not None
    assert isinstance(result.conversation_id, UUID)
    assert len(session.added) == 1
    assert session.flushed == 1


def test_get_conversation_returns_owned_conversation() -> None:
    conversation_id = uuid4()
    record = make_conversation_record(
        conversation_id=conversation_id,
    )

    session = FakeSession()
    session.scalar_result = record

    repository = PostgreSQLPrivateChatRepository(session)

    result = repository.get_conversation(
        conversation_id=conversation_id,
        user_id="user-1",
        world_id="polish_world",
    )

    assert result is not None
    assert result.conversation_id == conversation_id
    assert result.user_id == "user-1"
    assert result.world_id == "polish_world"


def test_get_conversation_returns_none_when_not_found() -> None:

    session.scalar_result = None

    repository = PostgreSQLPrivateChatRepository(session)

    result = repository.get_conversation(
        conversation_id=uuid4(),
        user_id="user-1",
        world_id="polish_world",
    )

    assert result is None


def test_save_message_persists_message_and_returns_domain_model() -> None:
    conversation_id = uuid4()
    created_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

    message = PrivateChatMessage(
        conversation_id=conversation_id,
        role="user",
        content="CzeÅ›Ä‡!",
        created_at=created_at,
    )

    session = FakeSession()
    repository = PostgreSQLPrivateChatRepository(session)

    result = repository.save_message(
        conversation_id=conversation_id,
        user_id="user-1",

    assert result.role == "user"
    assert result.content == "CzeÅ›Ä‡!"
    assert result.created_at == created_at

    assert len(session.added) == 1
    assert session.flushed == 1


def test_get_recent_messages_returns_chronological_order() -> None:
    conversation_id = uuid4()
    first_time = datetime(2026, 9, 14, 10, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    second_time = datetime(2026, 9, 14, 10, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    third_time = datetime(2026, 9, 14, 10, 2, tzinfo=timezone.utc)

    records = [
        type(
            "MessageRecord",
            (),
            {
                "conversation_id": conversation_id,
                "role": "assistant",
                "content": "Three",
                "created_at": third_time,
            },
        )(),
        type(
            "MessageRecord",
            (),
            {
                "conversation_id": conversation_id,
                "role": "user",
                "content": "Two",
                "created_at": second_time,
            },
        )(),
        type(
            "MessageRecord",
            (),
            {
                "conversation_id": conversation_id,
                "role": "user",
                "content": "One",
                "created_at": first_time,
            },
        )(),
    ]

    session = FakeSession()
    session.scalars_result = records

    repository = PostgreSQLPrivateChatRepository(session)

    result = repository.get_recent_messages(
        conversation_id=conversation_id,
        user_id="user-1",
        world_id="polish_world",
        limit=20,
    )

    assert [message.content for message in result] == [
        "One",
        "Two",
        "Three",
    ]


def test_get_recent_messages_rejects_invalid_limit() -> None:
    session = FakeSession()
    repository = PostgreSQLPrivateChatRepository(session)

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="limit must be greater than zero"):
        repository.get_recent_messages(
            conversation_id=uuid4(),
            user_id="user-1",
            world_id="polish_world",
            limit=0,
        )


def test_touch_conversation_updates_timestamp() -> None:
    conversation_id = uuid4()
    record = make_conversation_record(
        conversation_id=conversation_id,
    )

    previous_timestamp = record.updated_at

    session = FakeSession()
    session.scalar_result = record

    repository = PostgreSQLPrivateChatRepository(session)

    repository.touch_conversation(
        conversation_id=conversation_id,
        user_id="user-1",
        world_id="polish_world",
    )

    assert record.updated_at >= previous_timestamp
    assert session.flushed == 1

    repository = PostgreSQLPrivateChatRepository(session)

            conversation_id=uuid4(),
            user_id="user-1",

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from uuid import uuid4

import pytest
from dotenv import load_dotenv
from sqlalchemy import delete

from app.application.private_chat.models import PrivateChatMessage
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import (
    create_database_engine,
    create_session_factory,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.private_chat_models import (
    PrivateChatConversationRecord,
    PrivateChatMessageRecord,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.postgresql_private_chat_repository import (
    PostgreSQLPrivateChatRepository,
)


load_dotenv()

pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def session_factory():
    if not os.getenv("DATABASE_URL"):
        pytest.skip("DATABASE_URL is not configured")

    engine = create_database_engine()
    factory = create_session_factory(engine)

    yield factory

    engine.dispose()


def test_private_chat_repository_round_trip(
    session_factory,
) -> None:
    user_id = f"private-chat-test-{uuid4()}"
    world_id = "polish_world"

    session = session_factory()

    try:
        repository = PostgreSQLPrivateChatRepository(session)

        conversation = repository.create_conversation(
            user_id=user_id,
            world_id=world_id,
        )

        session.commit()

        assert conversation.conversation_id is not None
        assert conversation.user_id == user_id
        assert conversation.world_id == world_id

        loaded = repository.get_conversation(
            conversation_id=conversation.conversation_id,
            user_id=user_id,
            world_id=world_id,
        )

        assert loaded is not None
        assert loaded.conversation_id == conversation.conversation_id
        assert loaded.user_id == user_id
        assert loaded.world_id == world_id

        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        user_message = PrivateChatMessage(
            conversation_id=conversation.conversation_id,
            role="user",
            content="CzeÅ›Ä‡!",
            created_at=now,
        )

        assistant_message = PrivateChatMessage(
            conversation_id=conversation.conversation_id,
            role="assistant",
            content="CzeÅ›Ä‡! Jak mogÄ™ Ci pomÃ³c?",
            created_at=now,
        )

        repository.save_message(
            conversation_id=conversation.conversation_id,
            user_id=user_id,
            world_id=world_id,
            message=user_message,
        )

        repository.save_message(
            conversation_id=conversation.conversation_id,
            user_id=user_id,
            world_id=world_id,
            message=assistant_message,
        )

        repository.touch_conversation(
            conversation_id=conversation.conversation_id,
            user_id=user_id,
            world_id=world_id,
        )

        session.commit()

        messages = repository.get_recent_messages(
            conversation_id=conversation.conversation_id,
            user_id=user_id,
            world_id=world_id,
            limit=20,
        )

        assert len(messages) == 2
        assert messages[0].role == "user"
        assert messages[0].content == "CzeÅ›Ä‡!"
        assert messages[1].role == "assistant"
        assert messages[1].content == "CzeÅ›Ä‡! Jak mogÄ™ Ci pomÃ³c?"

    finally:
        conversation_id = locals().get("conversation", None)

        if conversation_id is not None:
            session.rollback()

            session.execute(
                delete(PrivateChatMessageRecord).where(
                    PrivateChatMessageRecord.conversation_id
                    == conversation_id.conversation_id
                )
            )

            session.execute(
                delete(PrivateChatConversationRecord).where(
                    PrivateChatConversationRecord.conversation_id
                    == conversation_id.conversation_id
                )
            )

            session.commit()

        session.close()

----- FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\infrastructure\persistence\test_postgresql_provider_usage_repository.py -----
from __future__ import annotations

import os
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from decimal import Decimal

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text

from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import (
    create_session_factory,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.postgresql_provider_usage_repository import (
    PostgreSQLProviderUsageRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.provider_usage_records import (
    ProviderUsageRecord,
)
from src.ai_runtime.providers.usage.models import ProviderUsageEvent


def _require_database_url() -> str:
    from dotenv import load_dotenv

    load_dotenv()

    value = os.getenv("DATABASE_URL")
    if not value or not value.startswith("postgresql"):
        pytest.skip("PostgreSQL DATABASE_URL is not configured.")
    return value


def _event() -> ProviderUsageEvent:
    return ProviderUsageEvent(
        provider="p1_4_test_provider",
        model="p1_4_test_model",
        capability="conversation",
        functionality="chat",
        agent="conversation_agent",
        input_tokens=11,
        output_tokens=7,
        latency_ms=123,
        estimated_cost=Decimal("0.00123456"),
        quality_score=91.5,
        success=True,
        created_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
    )


def test_repository_uses_canonical_session_boundary():
    url = _require_database_url()
    engine = create_engine(url, pool_pre_ping=True)
    try:
        session_factory = create_session_factory(engine)
        repository = PostgreSQLProviderUsageRepository(session_factory)
        assert repository._session_factory is session_factory
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_repository_round_trips_usage_event_against_postgresql():
    url = _require_database_url()
    engine = create_engine(url, pool_pre_ping=True)
    try:
        session_factory = create_session_factory(engine)
        repository = PostgreSQLProviderUsageRepository(session_factory)

        # P1.4.1 deliberately does not create/drop schema. The migration
        # is a separate P1.4.2 step.
        table_exists = engine.connect().execute(
            text(
                "SELECT to_regclass('public.provider_usage_events')"
            )
        ).scalar_one_or_none()

        if table_exists is None:
            pytest.skip(
                "provider_usage_events table is not migrated yet; "
                "P1.4.2 migration is intentionally separate."
            )

        repository.clear()
        event = _event()
        repository.save(event)

        rows = repository.get_all()
        assert len(rows) == 1
        restored = rows[0]
        assert restored.provider == event.provider
        assert restored.model == event.model
        assert restored.input_tokens == event.input_tokens
        assert restored.output_tokens == event.output_tokens
        assert restored.latency_ms == event.latency_ms
        assert restored.estimated_cost == event.estimated_cost
        assert restored.quality_score == event.quality_score
        assert restored.success is True
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from uuid import UUID, uuid4

import pytest

from app.domain.study_material.study_material import (
    StudyMaterialStatus,
    StudyMaterialType,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.postgresql_study_material_repository import (
    PostgreSQLStudyMaterialRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.study_material_models import (
    StudyMaterialRecord,
)


class FakeScalars:
    """Minimal SQLAlchemy scalars result double."""

    def __init__(self, records: list[object]) -> None:
        self._records = records

    def all(self) -> list[object]:
        return self._records


class FakeSession:
    """Minimal session double for repository unit tests."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.scalar_result: object | None = None
        self.scalars_result: list[object] = []
        self.statements: list[object] = []

    def __enter__(self) -> "FakeSession":
        return self

    def __exit__(
        self,
        exc_type: object,
        exc_value: object,
        traceback: object,
    ) -> None:
        return None

    def scalar(self, statement: object) -> object | None:
        self.statements.append(statement)
        return self.scalar_result

    def scalars(self, statement: object) -> FakeScalars:
        self.statements.append(statement)
        return FakeScalars(self.scalars_result)


class FakeSessionFactory:
    """Callable session factory matching the production boundary."""

    def __init__(self, session: FakeSession) -> None:
        self.session = session
        self.calls = 0

    def __call__(self) -> FakeSession:
        self.calls += 1
        return self.session


def make_record(
    *,
    material_id: UUID | None = None,
    title: str = "French Greetings",
    description: str | None = "Basic greetings in French.",
    material_type: str = "LESSON",
    status: str = "PUBLISHED",
    display_order: int = 0,
) -> StudyMaterialRecord:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

    return StudyMaterialRecord(
        material_id=material_id or uuid4(),
        world_id=uuid4(),
        title=title,
        description=description,
        material_type=material_type,
        status=status,
        display_order=display_order,
        created_at=now,
        updated_at=now,
    )


def test_repository_rejects_none_session_factory() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="session_factory cannot be None",
    ):
        PostgreSQLStudyMaterialRepository(None)  # type: ignore[arg-type]


def test_list_published_by_world_returns_domain_models() -> None:
    first = make_record(
        title="French Greetings",
        material_type="LESSON",
        status="PUBLISHED",
        display_order=0,
    )
    second = make_record(
        title="Everyday Vocabulary",
        material_type="READING",
        status="PUBLISHED",
        display_order=1,
    )

    session = FakeSession()
    session.scalars_result = [first, second]

    factory = FakeSessionFactory(session)
    repository = PostgreSQLStudyMaterialRepository(factory)

    result = repository.list_published_by_world(
        "french_world",
    )

    assert factory.calls == 1
    assert len(result) == 2

    assert result[0].material_id == first.material_id
    assert result[0].world_id == "french_world"
    assert result[0].title == "French Greetings"
    assert result[0].description == "Basic greetings in French."
    assert result[0].material_type is StudyMaterialType.LESSON
    assert result[0].status is StudyMaterialStatus.PUBLISHED
    assert result[0].display_order == 0

    assert result[1].material_id == second.material_id
    assert result[1].world_id == "french_world"
    assert result[1].title == "Everyday Vocabulary"
    assert result[1].material_type is StudyMaterialType.READING
    assert result[1].status is StudyMaterialStatus.PUBLISHED
    assert result[1].display_order == 1

def test_repository_preserves_material_timestamps() -> None:
    created_at = datetime(
        2026,
        9,
        17,
        12,
        0,
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    )
    updated_at = datetime(
        2026,
        9,
        17,
        13,
        0,
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    )

    record = StudyMaterialRecord(
        material_id=uuid4(),
        world_id=uuid4(),
        title="French Lesson",
        description="Lesson description",
        material_type="LESSON",
        status="PUBLISHED",
        display_order=5,
        created_at=created_at,
        updated_at=updated_at,
    )

    session = FakeSession()
    session.scalar_result = record

    factory = FakeSessionFactory(session)
    repository = PostgreSQLStudyMaterialRepository(factory)

    result = repository.get_published(
        material_id=record.material_id,
        world_id="french_world",
    )

    assert result is not None
    assert result.created_at == created_at
    assert result.updated_at == updated_at

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
from pathlib import Path

from sqlalchemy import BigInteger, Boolean, DateTime, Integer, Numeric, String


MIGRATION = (
    Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
    / "alembic"
    / "versions"
    / "20260907_provider_usage_events.py"
)


def _load_migration():
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
        "provider_usage_migration",
        MIGRATION,
    )
    assert spec is not None
    assert spec.loader is not None
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def test_provider_usage_migration_isolated_and_explicit() -> None:
    migration = _load_migration()

    assert migration.revision == "20260907_provider_usage_events"
    assert migration.down_revision is None
    assert migration.branch_labels == ("provider_usage_ledger",)
    assert migration.depends_on is None


def test_provider_usage_record_model_matches_migration_contract() -> None:
    from app.infrastructure.persistence.provider_usage_records import (
        ProviderUsageRecord,
    )

    columns = ProviderUsageRecord.__table__.columns

    assert isinstance(columns["id"].type, BigInteger)

    assert isinstance(columns["provider"].type, String)
    assert columns["provider"].type.length == 64

    assert isinstance(columns["model"].type, String)
    assert columns["model"].type.length == 128

    assert isinstance(columns["input_tokens"].type, Integer)
    assert isinstance(columns["output_tokens"].type, Integer)
    assert isinstance(columns["latency_ms"].type, Integer)

    assert isinstance(columns["estimated_cost"].type, Numeric)
    assert columns["estimated_cost"].type.precision == 18
    assert columns["estimated_cost"].type.scale == 8

    assert isinstance(columns["quality_score"].type, Numeric)
    assert columns["quality_score"].type.precision == 5
    assert columns["quality_score"].type.scale == 2

    assert isinstance(columns["success"].type, Boolean)

    assert isinstance(columns["created_at"].type, DateTime)
    assert columns["created_at"].type.timezone is True
